```
DANHAYS | The Home of Melanin Beauty
Mission: To celebrate and serve melanin-rich skin with the world's best curated beauty products
Platform Metrics:
+-- 50+ Partner Brands (Black-owned, African, Inclusive)
+-- 2,500+ Products
+-- 500+ Shades (Foundation, Concealer, etc.)
+-- 51 Languages (38 African + 13 European)
+-- 174+ Payment Methods (Including M-Pesa, Paystack, Flutterwave)
+-- 11 Currencies (EUR, GBP, USD, ZAR, NGN, KES, etc.)
+-- 190+ Countries Served
+-- 500,000+ Community Members
+-- 5,000+ Creators
+-- 125,000+ Reviews
+-- 4.9/5 Average Rating
+-- 98% AI Shade Match Accuracy

Key Features:
+-- AI Shade Finder (Cross-brand matching)

## PORTION 2: HTML DOCUMENT STRUCTURE & HEAD SECTION--
### 2.1 HTML Root Element
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html
    lang="{{config.language}}"
    dir="{{config.direction}}"
    data-theme="light"
    data-currency="{{user.currency | default: config.currency}}"
    data-country="{{user.country | default: config.country}}"
    data-language="{{user.language | default: config.language}}"
    data-user-logged="{{user.isLoggedIn | default: false}}"
    data-user-id="{{user.id | default: ''}}"
    data-user-tier="{{user.tier | default: 'guest'}}"
    data-user-shade-profile="{{user.shadeProfile | default: ''}}"
    data-cart-count="{{cart.itemCount | default: 0}}"
    data-cart-total="{{cart.total | default: 0}}"
    data-wishlist-count="{{wishlist.count | default: 0}}"
    data-compare-count="{{compare.count | default: 0}}"
    data-recently-viewed="{{recentlyViewed.count | default: 0}}"
    data-reduced-motion="{{user.preferences.reducedMotion | default: false}}"
    data-high-contrast="{{user.preferences.highContrast | default: false}}"
    data-font-size="{{user.preferences.fontSize | default: 100}}"
    data-dyslexia-font="{{user.preferences.dyslexiaFont | default: false}}"
    data-color-blind="{{user.preferences.colorBlindMode | default: 'none'}}"
    data-store-mode="{{isStoreMode | default: false}}"
    data-page-type="{{page.type}}"
    data-page-template="{{page.template}}"
    data-app-version="{{config.version}}"
    data-build-date="{{config.buildDate}}"
    data-environment="{{config.environment}}"
    itemscope
    itemtype="https://schema.org/WebPage"
>
```--
### 2.2 Head Section — Document Meta
```html
<head>
    <!-- ============================================
         CHARACTER ENCODING & VIEWPORT
    ============================================ -->
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=5.0, user-scalable=yes,
viewport-fit=cover">
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=edge">
    <!-- ============================================
         SECURITY HEADERS
    ============================================ -->
    <meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="
        default-src 'self';
        script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval'
            https://cdn.danhays.com
            https://www.googletagmanager.com
            https://www.google-analytics.com
            https://connect.facebook.net
            https://analytics.tiktok.com
            https://js.stripe.com
            https://js.klarna.com
            https://www.paypal.com
            https://maps.googleapis.com;
        style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'
            https://cdn.danhays.com
            https://fonts.googleapis.com;
        img-src 'self' data: blob:
            https://cdn.danhays.com
            https://*.googleusercontent.com
            https://*.facebook.com
            https://*.instagram.com;
        font-src 'self'
            https://cdn.danhays.com
            https://fonts.gstatic.com;
        connect-src 'self'
            https://api.danhays.com
            https://*.google-analytics.com
            https://*.facebook.com
            wss://ws.danhays.com;
        frame-src 'self'
            https://js.stripe.com
            https://www.youtube.com
            https://player.vimeo.com;
        media-src 'self'
            https://cdn.danhays.com
            blob:;
        object-src 'none';
        base-uri 'self';
        form-action 'self'
            https://api.danhays.com;
        frame-ancestors 'self';
        upgrade-insecure-requests;
    ">
    <meta http-equiv="X-Content-Type-Options" content="nosniff">
    <meta http-equiv="X-Frame-Options" content="SAMEORIGIN">
    <meta http-equiv="X-XSS-Protection" content="1; mode=block">
    <meta name="referrer" content="strict-origin-when-cross-origin">
    <meta http-equiv="Permissions-Policy" content="
        camera=(self),
        microphone=(self),
        geolocation=(self),
        payment=(self),
        usb=(),
        magnetometer=(),
        gyroscope=(self),
        accelerometer=(self),
        interest-cohort=()
    ">
    <!-- ============================================
         THEME COLOR (Dynamic for Light/Dark)
    ============================================ -->
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#D4AF37" media="(prefers-color-scheme: light)">
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#1A1A2E" media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)">
    <meta name="color-scheme" content="light dark">
    <!-- ============================================
         PAGE TITLE (Dynamic per page type)
    ============================================ -->
    <!-- Homepage -->
    {{#if page.isHomepage}}
    <title>{{config.business.name}} | {{config.business.tagline}} | {{config.platform.brandCount}}+ Brands for Melan
in Beauty</title>
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Product Page -->
    {{#if page.isProduct}}
    <title>{{product.name}} | {{product.brand.name}} | {{config.business.name}}</title>
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Collection Page -->
    {{#if page.isCollection}}
    <title>{{collection.name}} | Shop {{collection.productCount}}+ Products | {{config.business.name}}</title>
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Brand Page -->
    {{#if page.isBrand}}
    <title>{{brand.name}} | Shop {{brand.productCount}}+ Products | {{config.business.name}}</title>
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Search Results -->
    {{#if page.isSearch}}
    <title>Search: "{{search.query}}" | {{search.resultCount}} Results | {{config.business.name}}</title>
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Account Pages -->
    {{#if page.isAccount}}
    <title>{{page.title}} | My Account | {{config.business.name}}</title>
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Cart -->
    {{#if page.isCart}}
    <title>Shopping Bag ({{cart.itemCount}}) | {{config.business.name}}</title>
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Checkout -->
    {{#if page.isCheckout}}
    <title>Checkout | {{config.business.name}}</title>
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Generic Pages -->
    {{#if page.isGeneric}}
    <title>{{page.title}} | {{config.business.name}}</title>
    {{/if}}
    <!-- ============================================
         SEO META TAGS
    ============================================ -->
    <meta name="description" content="{{page.metaDescription | default: config.seo.defaultDescription}}">
    <meta name="keywords" content="{{page.keywords | default: 'melanin beauty, Black-owned beauty brands, inclusi
ve cosmetics, African beauty, brown skin makeup, shade matching'}}">
    <meta name="author" content="{{config.business.name}}">
    <meta name="copyright" content="© {{currentYear}} {{config.business.legalName}}. All rights reserved.">
    <meta name="generator" content="DANHAYS Platform {{config.version}}">
    <!-- Robots -->
    {{#if page.noIndex}}
    <meta name="robots" content="noindex, nofollow">
    {{else}}
    <meta name="robots" content="index, follow, max-image-preview:large, max-snippet:-1, max-video-preview:-1">
    {{/if}}
    <meta name="googlebot" content="index, follow, max-snippet:-1, max-image-preview:large, max-video-preview:
1">
    <meta name="bingbot" content="index, follow">
    <!-- Additional SEO -->
    <meta name="revisit-after" content="1 days">
    <meta name="rating" content="general">
    <meta name="distribution" content="global">
    <meta name="coverage" content="worldwide">
    <meta name="target" content="all">
    <meta name="HandheldFriendly" content="true">
    <meta name="MobileOptimized" content="width">
    <!-- Format Detection (Disable auto-linking) -->
    <meta name="format-detection" content="telephone=no, email=no, address=no, date=no">
    <!-- ============================================
         DUBLIN CORE METADATA
    ============================================ -->

    <meta name="DC.creator" content="{{config.business.legalName}}">
    <meta name="DC.subject" content="Beauty, Cosmetics, Melanin, Inclusive Beauty, Skincare, Makeup">

### 2.4 Head Section — Open Graph Meta
```html
    <!-- ============================================
         OPEN GRAPH META (Facebook, LinkedIn, etc.)
    ============================================ -->
    <meta property="og:type" content="{{page.ogType | default: 'website'}}">
    <meta property="og:site_name" content="{{config.business.name}}">
    <meta property="og:title" content="{{page.ogTitle | default: page.title}}">
    <meta property="og:description" content="{{page.ogDescription | default: page.metaDescription}}">
    <meta property="og:url" content="{{page.canonicalUrl}}">
    <meta property="og:locale" content="{{config.locale | default: 'en_US'}}">
    <!-- OG Locale Alternates -->
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="en_GB">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="nl_NL">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="nl_BE">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="fr_FR">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="fr_BE">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="de_DE">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="es_ES">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="pt_PT">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="pt_BR">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="it_IT">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="ar_EG">
    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content="sw_KE">
    <!-- OG Images -->
    <meta property="og:image" content="{{page.ogImage | default: config.seo.defaultImage}}">
    <meta property="og:image:secure_url" content="{{page.ogImage | default: config.seo.defaultImage}}">
    <meta property="og:image:type" content="image/jpeg">
    <meta property="og:image:width" content="1200">
    <meta property="og:image:height" content="630">
    <meta property="og:image:alt" content="{{page.ogImageAlt | default: page.title}}">
    <!-- Square Image for some platforms -->
    <meta property="og:image" content="{{page.ogImageSquare | default: 'https://cdn.danhays.com/images/og/og-squa
re.jpg'}}">
    <meta property="og:image:width" content="1200">
    <meta property="og:image:height" content="1200">
    <!-- Story/Vertical Image -->
    <meta property="og:image" content="{{page.ogImageStory | default: 'https://cdn.danhays.com/images/og/og-sto
ry.jpg'}}">
    <meta property="og:image:width" content="1080">
    <meta property="og:image:height" content="1920">
    <!-- OG Video (if applicable) -->
    {{#if page.ogVideo}}
    <meta property="og:video" content="{{page.ogVideo}}">
    <meta property="og:video:secure_url" content="{{page.ogVideo}}">
    <meta property="og:video:type" content="video/mp4">
    <meta property="og:video:width" content="1920">
    <meta property="og:video:height" content="1080">
    {{/if}}
    <!-- OG Additional -->
    <meta property="og:determiner" content="the">
    <meta property="og:updated_time" content="{{page.lastModified | default: config.lastUpdated}}">
    <!-- Facebook Specific -->
    <meta property="fb:app_id" content="{{config.analytics.facebookAppId}}">
    <meta property="fb:pages" content="{{config.analytics.facebookPageId}}">
    <!-- ============================================
         PRODUCT-SPECIFIC OG (When on product page)
    ============================================ -->
    {{#if page.isProduct}}
    <meta property="og:type" content="product">
    <meta property="product:brand" content="{{product.brand.name}}">
    <meta property="product:availability" content="{{#if product.inStock}}in stock{{else}}out of stock{{/if}}">
    <meta property="product:condition" content="new">
    <meta property="product:price:amount" content="{{product.price}}">
    <meta property="product:price:currency" content="{{config.currency}}">
    <meta property="product:retailer_item_id" content="{{product.sku}}">
    <meta property="product:item_group_id" content="{{product.id}}">
    {{#if product.compareAtPrice}}
    <meta property="product:sale_price:amount" content="{{product.price}}">
    <meta property="product:sale_price:currency" content="{{config.currency}}">
    <meta property="product:original_price:amount" content="{{product.compareAtPrice}}">
    <meta property="product:original_price:currency" content="{{config.currency}}">
    {{/if}}
    <meta property="product:category" content="{{product.category}}">
    {{#each product.images}}
    <meta property="og:image" content="{{this.url}}">
    {{/each}}
    {{/if}}
    <!-- ============================================
         ARTICLE-SPECIFIC OG (When on blog/article)
    ============================================ -->
    {{#if page.isArticle}}
    <meta property="og:type" content="article">
    <meta property="article:published_time" content="{{article.publishedAt}}">
    <meta property="article:modified_time" content="{{article.updatedAt}}">
    <meta property="article:author" content="{{article.author.name}}">
    <meta property="article:section" content="{{article.category}}">
    {{#each article.tags}}
    <meta property="article:tag" content="{{this}}">
    {{/each}}
    {{/if}}
```--
### 2.5 Head Section — Twitter Cards
```html
    <!-- ============================================
         TWITTER CARD META
    ============================================ -->
    <meta name="twitter:card" content="{{page.twitterCard | default: 'summary_large_image'}}">
    <meta name="twitter:site" content="@{{config.social.twitter.handle}}">
    <meta name="twitter:creator" content="@{{config.social.twitter.handle}}">
    <meta name="twitter:title" content="{{page.twitterTitle | default: page.title}}">
    <meta name="twitter:description" content="{{page.twitterDescription | default: page.metaDescription}}">
    <meta name="twitter:image" content="{{page.twitterImage | default: page.ogImage}}">
    <meta name="twitter:image:alt" content="{{page.twitterImageAlt | default: page.title}}">
    <!-- Twitter Player (for video content) -->
    {{#if page.twitterVideo}}
    <meta name="twitter:card" content="player">
    <meta name="twitter:player" content="{{page.twitterVideoPlayer}}">
    <meta name="twitter:player:width" content="1280">
    <meta name="twitter:player:height" content="720">
    <meta name="twitter:player:stream" content="{{page.twitterVideo}}">
    <meta name="twitter:player:stream:content_type" content="video/mp4">
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Twitter App Cards -->
    <meta name="twitter:app:name:iphone" content="{{config.business.name}}">
    <meta name="twitter:app:id:iphone" content="{{config.apps.ios.id}}">
    <meta name="twitter:app:url:iphone" content="{{config.apps.deepLinkScheme}}{{page.path}}">
    <meta name="twitter:app:name:ipad" content="{{config.business.name}}">
    <meta name="twitter:app:id:ipad" content="{{config.apps.ios.id}}">
    <meta name="twitter:app:url:ipad" content="{{config.apps.deepLinkScheme}}{{page.path}}">
    <meta name="twitter:app:name:googleplay" content="{{config.business.name}}">
    <meta name="twitter:app:id:googleplay" content="{{config.apps.android.id}}">
    <meta name="twitter:app:url:googleplay" content="{{config.apps.deepLinkScheme}}{{page.path}}">
    <!-- ============================================
         PLATFORM-SPECIFIC META TAGS
    ============================================ -->
    <!-- Instagram / Facebook App Links -->
    <meta property="al:ios:app_store_id" content="{{config.apps.ios.id}}">
    <meta property="al:ios:app_name" content="{{config.business.name}}">
    <meta property="al:ios:url" content="{{config.apps.deepLinkScheme}}{{page.path}}">
    <meta property="al:android:package" content="{{config.apps.android.id}}">
    <meta property="al:android:app_name" content="{{config.business.name}}">
    <meta property="al:android:url" content="{{config.apps.deepLinkScheme}}{{page.path}}">
    <meta property="al:web:url" content="{{page.canonicalUrl}}">
    <meta property="al:web:should_fallback" content="true">
    <!-- TikTok -->
    <meta name="tiktok:creator" content="@{{config.social.tiktok.handle}}">
    <meta name="tiktok:app_id" content="{{config.analytics.tiktokPixelId}}">
    <!-- Snapchat -->
    <meta name="snapchat:sticker_asset" content="{{config.seo.defaultImage}}">
    <meta name="snapchat:publisher_id" content="{{config.analytics.snapchatId}}">
    <!-- Pinterest -->
    <meta name="pinterest" content="nopin" description="{{page.metaDescription}}">
    <meta name="pinterest:rich_pin" content="true">
    <meta name="pinterest:description" content="{{page.metaDescription}}">
    <meta name="pinterest:media" content="{{page.ogImage}}">
    <!-- LinkedIn -->
    <meta name="linkedin:owner" content="{{config.social.linkedin.url}}">
    <!-- WhatsApp -->
    <meta property="og:whatsapp:catalog_id" content="{{config.whatsapp.catalogId}}">
    <meta property="og:whatsapp:phone" content="{{config.social.whatsapp.number}}">
```--

### 2.7 Head Section — Preconnect, DNS Prefetch, Preload
```html
    <!-- ============================================
         PRECONNECT (Critical Third-Party Origins)
    ============================================ -->
    <!-- Fonts -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <!-- CDN -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://cdn.danhays.com" crossorigin>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://static.danhays.com" crossorigin>
    <!-- API -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://api.danhays.com" crossorigin>
    <!-- Analytics -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://www.googletagmanager.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://www.google-analytics.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://connect.facebook.net">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://analytics.tiktok.com">
    <!-- Payments -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://js.stripe.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://js.klarna.com">
    <!-- Maps -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://maps.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://maps.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <!-- ============================================
         DNS PREFETCH (Non-Critical Origins)
    ============================================ -->
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//cdn.shopify.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//images.danhays.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//video.danhays.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//www.paypal.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//www.klarna.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//api.afterpay.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//api.paystack.co">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//js.paystack.co">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//api.flutterwave.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//widget.trustpilot.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//static.hotjar.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//cdn.segment.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//api.instagram.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//graph.facebook.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//api.tiktok.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//www.youtube.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//i.ytimg.com">
    <link rel="dns-prefetch" href="//player.vimeo.com">
    <!-- ============================================
         PRELOAD (Critical Resources)
    ============================================ -->
    <!-- Critical CSS -->
    <link rel="preload" href="/css/critical.css" as="style">
    <!-- Main Stylesheet -->
    <link rel="preload" href="/css/main.css" as="style">
    <!-- Core Fonts -->
    <link rel="preload" href="/fonts/DMSans-Variable.woff2" as="font" type="font/woff2" crossorigin>
    <link rel="preload" href="/fonts/PlayfairDisplay-Variable.woff2" as="font" type="font/woff2" crossorigin>
    <!-- Core JavaScript -->
    <link rel="preload" href="/js/core/app.js" as="script">
    <!-- Logo -->
    <link rel="preload" href="/images/logo/logo.svg" as="image" type="image/svg+xml">
    <!-- Icon Sprite -->
    <link rel="preload" href="/images/icons/sprite.svg" as="image" type="image/svg+xml">
    <!-- Hero Image (Homepage only) -->
    {{#if page.isHomepage}}
    <link rel="preload" href="/images/hero/hero-main.webp" as="image" type="image/webp" fetchpriority="high" me
dia="(min-width: 768px)">
    <link rel="preload" href="/images/hero/hero-main-mobile.webp" as="image" type="image/webp" fetchpriori
ty="high" media="(max-width: 767px)">
    {{/if}}
    <!-- Product Image (Product page only) -->
    {{#if page.isProduct}}
    <link rel="preload" href="{{product.images[0].url}}" as="image" fetchpriority="high">
    {{/if}}
    <!-- ============================================
         MODULEPRELOAD (ES Modules)
    ============================================ -->
    <link rel="modulepreload" href="/js/core/state.js">
    <link rel="modulepreload" href="/js/core/router.js">
    <link rel="modulepreload" href="/js/core/events.js">
    <link rel="modulepreload" href="/js/core/api.js">
    <link rel="modulepreload" href="/js/components/index.js">
    <!-- ============================================
         PREFETCH (Likely Next Pages)
    ============================================ -->
    <link rel="prefetch" href="/collections/all">
    <link rel="prefetch" href="/brands">
    <link rel="prefetch" href="/tools/shade-finder">
    <link rel="prefetch" href="/tools/virtual-tryon">

    <!-- ============================================
         PRERENDER (Very Likely Next Page)
    ============================================ -->

    <link rel="prerender" href="/collections/bestsellers">
    {{/if}}
    {{#if page.isCollection}}
    <link rel="prerender" href="{{collection.firstProduct.url}}">
    {{/if}}

    <script type="application/ld+json">

        },

        },

        },

            "@type": "SpeakableSpecification",

                ".hero-headline",

### 2.10 Head Section — Critical CSS (Inline)
```html
    <!-- ============================================
         CRITICAL CSS (Inline for performance)
    ============================================ -->
    <style id="critical-css">
        /* ========================================
           RESET & BASE
        ======================================== */
        *, *::before, *::after {
            box-sizing: border-box;
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
        }
        html {
            scroll-behavior: smooth;
            text-size-adjust: 100%;
            -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%;
            -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
            font-size: 100%;
        }
        @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
            html {
                scroll-behavior: auto;
            }

### 2.11 Head Section — No-JS Fallback Styles
```html
    <!-- ============================================
         NO-JS FALLBACK STYLES
    ============================================ -->
    <noscript>
        <style>
            /* Show content without JS animations */
            .preloader { display: none !important; }
            [data-aos] { opacity: 1 !important; transform: none !important; }
            .lazy-load { opacity: 1; }
            /* Mobile menu fallback */
            .mobile-menu { display: none; }
            .nav-main { display: flex !important; }
            /* Carousel fallback - show as grid */
            .swiper-wrapper {
                display: grid;
                grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(250px, 1fr));
                gap: 1rem;
            }
            .swiper-slide { width: auto !important; }
            .swiper-button-prev,
            .swiper-button-next,
            .swiper-pagination { display: none; }
            /* Tabs fallback - show all content */
            .tab-panel[hidden] { display: block !important; }
            .tab-panel::before {
                content: attr(aria-labelledby);
                display: block;
                font-weight: bold;
                margin-bottom: 1rem;
            }
            /* Modal fallback */
            .modal { display: none; }
            /* Form validation fallback */
            input:invalid { border-color: #EF4444; }
            input:valid { border-color: #10B981; }
        </style>
    </noscript>
```--

## PORTION 3: BODY OPENING, PRELOADER, SKIP LINKS & ACCESSIBILITY (Continued)--
### 3.2 Skip Links (Accessibility)
```html
    <!-- ============================================
         SKIP LINKS (Accessibility Navigation)
    ============================================ -->
    <div class="skip-links" id="skipLinks">
        <!-- Primary Skip Link -->
        <a href="#main-content" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to main content
        </a>
        <!-- Navigation Skip Link -->
        <a href="#main-navigation" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to navigation
        </a>
        <!-- Search Skip Link -->
        <a href="#search" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to search
        </a>
        <!-- Footer Skip Link -->
        <a href="#footer" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to footer
        </a>
        <!-- Page-Specific Skip Links (Conditional) -->
        {{#if page.isProduct}}
        <a href="#product-info" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to product information
        </a>
        <a href="#product-reviews" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to reviews
        </a>
        {{/if}}
        {{#if page.isCollection}}
        <a href="#products-grid" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to products
        </a>
        <a href="#filters" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to filters
        </a>
        {{/if}}
        {{#if page.isBrand}}
        <a href="#brand-products" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to products
        </a>
        <a href="#brand-story" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to brand story
        </a>
        {{/if}}
        {{#if page.isCart}}
        <a href="#cart-items" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to cart items
        </a>
        <a href="#cart-summary" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to order summary
        </a>
        {{/if}}
        {{#if page.isCheckout}}
        <a href="#checkout-form" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to checkout form
        </a>
        <a href="#order-summary" class="skip-link" data-skip-link>
            Skip to order summary
        </a>
        {{/if}}
    </div>

### 3.3 Preloader
```html
    <!-- ============================================
         PRELOADER
    ============================================ -->
    <div
        class="preloader"
        id="preloader"
        role="progressbar"
        aria-label="Page loading"
        aria-valuetext="Loading page content"
        aria-busy="true"
    >
        <div class="preloader-inner">
            <!-- Logo -->

                    src="/images/logo/logo-gold.svg"
                    alt="{{config.business.name}}"

                    height="48"
                    class="preloader-logo-image"
                >
            </div>

                <svg class="preloader-spinner-svg" viewBox="0 0 50 50">

                        class="preloader-spinner-track"
                        cx="25"
                        cy="25"

                        fill="none"
                        stroke-width="4"
                    ></circle>

                        cx="25"
                        cy="25"
                        r="20"

                        stroke-dashoffset="126"
                    ></circle>
                </svg>
            </div>
            <!-- Loading Text -->
            <p class="preloader-text">
                <span class="preloader-text-loading">Loading</span>

                    <span class="preloader-dot">.</span>

                    <span class="preloader-dot">.</span>
                </span>
            </p>

                <div class="preloader-progress-track">
                    <div class="preloader-progress-bar" id="preloaderProgressBar" style="width: 0%"></div>
                </div>

            </div>
        </div>
        <!-- Background Pattern -->
        <div class="preloader-background" aria-hidden="true">
            <div class="preloader-pattern"></div>
        </div>
    </div>
    <!-- Preloader Styles (Inline for immediate rendering) -->
    <style id="preloader-styles">
        .preloader {
            position: fixed;
            inset: 0;

            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;

        }

            opacity: 0;
            visibility: hidden;
            pointer-events: none;
        }
        .preloader.is-complete {
            transition-delay: 0.2s;
        }
        .preloader-inner {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            align-items: center;

            gap: 1.5rem;
            text-align: center;

        }
        .preloader-logo {
            animation: preloader-pulse 2s ease-in-out infinite;
        }
        .preloader-logo-image {
            width: 120px;
            height: auto;
        }

            0%, 100% {
                opacity: 1;
                transform: scale(1);
            }
            50% {
                opacity: 0.7;

            }
        }

            width: 50px;
            height: 50px;
        }
        .preloader-spinner-svg {
            animation: preloader-rotate 2s linear infinite;
        }
        @keyframes preloader-rotate {
            100% { transform: rotate(360deg); }
        }
        .preloader-spinner-track {
            stroke: var(--color-gray-200, #E5E7EB);
        }
        [data-theme="dark"] .preloader-spinner-track {
            stroke: var(--color-gray-700, #374151);
        }
        .preloader-spinner-fill {
            stroke: var(--color-gold, #D4AF37);
            stroke-linecap: round;
            animation: preloader-dash 1.5s ease-in-out infinite;
        }
        @keyframes preloader-dash {
            0% {
                stroke-dashoffset: 126;
            }
            50% {
                stroke-dashoffset: 32;
            }
            100% {
                stroke-dashoffset: 126;
            }
        }
        .preloader-text {
            font-family: var(--font-primary, 'DM Sans', sans-serif);
            font-size: 0.875rem;
            font-weight: 500;
            color: var(--color-text-secondary, #6B7280);
            letter-spacing: 0.1em;
            text-transform: uppercase;

        .preloader-dots {

                        <div class="announcement-language-selector" data-dropdown>
                            <button
                                type="button"

                                data-dropdown-trigger

                            >

                            </button>

                                    <span>Select Language</span>

                                            <span class="language-flag">{{this.flag}}</span>
                                            <span class="language-name">{{this.nativeName}}</span>
                                        </a>
                                    </li>
                                    {{/each}}

                                        View all {{config.platform.languageCount}} languages
                                        <svg class="icon icon-sm"><use href="#icon-arrow-right"></use></svg>
                                    </a>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <span class="announcement-divider" aria-hidden="true">|</span>
                        <!-- Currency Selector -->
                        <div class="announcement-currency-selector" data-dropdown>
                            <button
                                type="button"

                                data-dropdown-trigger
                                aria-expanded="false"

                            >

de}}</span>

                            </button>

                                    {{#each currencies}}

                                            type="button"
                                            class="currency-option {{#if this.isCurrent}}is-active{{/if}}"
                                            data-action="set-currency"
                                            data-currency="{{this.code}}"
                                        >
                                            <span class="currency-symbol">{{this.symbol}}</span>
                                            <span class="currency-code">{{this.code}}</span>
                                            <span class="currency-name">{{this.name}}</span>
                                        </button>
                                    </li>
                                    {{/each}}
                                </ul>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

### 4.4 Search Drawer
```html
        <!-- ============================================
             SEARCH DRAWER
        ============================================ -->
        <div
            class="search-drawer"
            id="searchDrawer"
            role="dialog"
            aria-labelledby="searchDrawerTitle"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-hidden="true"
            hidden
        >
            <div class="search-drawer-backdrop" data-action="close-search"></div>
            <div class="search-drawer-content">
                <!-- Search Header -->
                <div class="search-drawer-header">
                    <h2 class="search-drawer-title visually-hidden" id="searchDrawerTitle">
                        Search {{config.business.name}}
                    </h2>
                    <form
                        class="search-form"
                        id="searchForm"
                        action="/search"
                        method="get"
                        role="search"
                    >
                        <div class="search-input-wrapper">
                            <svg class="icon search-input-icon" aria-hidden="true">
                                <use href="#icon-search"></use>
                            </svg>
                            <input
                                type="search"
                                id="searchInput"
                                name="q"
                                class="search-input"
                                placeholder="Search products, brands, ingredients..."
                                autocomplete="off"
                                autocapitalize="off"
                                autocorrect="off"
                                spellcheck="false"
                                aria-label="Search"
                                aria-describedby="searchHint"
                                aria-autocomplete="list"
                                aria-controls="searchResults"
                                data-search-input
                            >
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                class="search-clear-btn"
                                data-action="clear-search"
                                aria-label="Clear search"
                                hidden
                            >
                                <svg class="icon"><use href="#icon-x"></use></svg>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        <span id="searchHint" class="visually-hidden">
                            Type to search. Results will appear below.
                        </span>
                    </form>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        class="search-drawer-close"
                        data-action="close-search"
                        aria-label="Close search"
                    >
                        <svg class="icon"><use href="#icon-x"></use></svg>
                    </button>
                </div>
                <!-- Search Filters -->
                <div class="search-filters" id="searchFilters">
                    <div class="search-filters-scroll">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            class="search-filter-btn is-active"
                            data-search-filter="all"
                        >
                            All
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            class="search-filter-btn"
                            data-search-filter="products"
                        >
                            Products

                        <button

                    <div class="search-initial" id="searchInitial">
                        <!-- Recent Searches -->
                        {{#if user.recentSearches}}
                        <div class="search-section" id="recentSearches">

                                        aria-label="Remove {{this.query}} from recent searches"
                                    >
                                        <svg class="icon icon-sm"><use href="#icon-x"></use></svg>

                                    href="/search?q={{this.query}}"
                                    class="search-trending-tag"
                                    data-search-trending="{{this.query}}"
                                >
                                    {{this.query}}
                                    {{#if this.isHot}}
                                    <span class="search-trending-hot">  </span>
                                    {{/if}}
                                </a>
                                {{/each}}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <!-- Popular Categories -->
                        <div class="search-section" id="popularCategories">
                            <h3 class="search-section-title">Popular Categories</h3>
                            <div class="search-categories-grid">
                                <a href="/collections/foundation" class="search-category-card">

                                </a>

                                    <img
                                        src="/images/categories/skincare-thumb.jpg"
                                        alt=""
                                        loading="lazy"

                                        height="60"
                                    >
                                    <span>Skincare</span>
                                </a>

### 4.6 Cart Drawer
```html
        <!-- ============================================
             CART DRAWER
        ============================================ -->
        <div
            class="cart-drawer"
            id="cartDrawer"
            role="dialog"
            aria-labelledby="cartDrawerTitle"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-hidden="true"
            hidden
        >
            <div class="cart-drawer-backdrop" data-action="close-cart"></div>
            <aside class="cart-drawer-panel">
                <!-- Cart Header -->
                <div class="cart-drawer-header">
                    <h2 class="cart-drawer-title" id="cartDrawerTitle">
                        <svg class="icon"><use href="#icon-bag"></use></svg>
                        Shopping Bag
                        <span class="cart-drawer-count" id="cartDrawerCount">
                            ({{cart.itemCount}} {{#if cart.itemCount === 1}}item{{else}}items{{/if}})
                        </span>
                    </h2>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        class="cart-drawer-close"
                        data-action="close-cart"
                        aria-label="Close cart"
                    >
                        <svg class="icon"><use href="#icon-x"></use></svg>
                    </button>
                </div>
                <!-- Free Shipping Progress -->
                <div class="cart-shipping-progress" id="cartShippingProgress">
                    {{#if cart.hasFreeShipping}}
                    <div class="cart-shipping-complete">
                        <svg class="icon"><use href="#icon-check-circle"></use></svg>
                        <span>You've unlocked <strong>FREE shipping!</strong></span>
                    </div>
                    {{else}}
                    <div class="cart-shipping-needed">
                        <p class="cart-shipping-text">
                            Add <strong>€{{cart.amountToFreeShipping}}</strong> more for
                            <strong>FREE shipping</strong>
                        </p>
                        <div class="cart-shipping-bar">
                            <div
                                class="cart-shipping-bar-fill"
                                style="width: {{cart.freeShippingProgress}}%"
                                role="progressbar"
                                aria-valuenow="{{cart.freeShippingProgress}}"
                                aria-valuemin="0"
                                aria-valuemax="100"
                            ></div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    {{/if}}
                </div>
                <!-- Cart Body -->
                <div class="cart-drawer-body" id="cartDrawerBody">
                    {{#if cart.itemCount}}

                    <ul class="cart-items" id="cartItems" role="list">
                        {{#each cart.items}}
                        <li
                            class="cart-item"
                            data-cart-item

                        >

                                        height="80"
                                    >
                                </a>

                                    {{/each}}
                                </div>
                                {{/if}}
                            </div>
                            <!-- Item Info -->

                                        {{this.brand.name}}
                                    </a>
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        class="cart-item-remove"
                                        data-action="remove-cart-item"
                                        data-line-id="{{this.lineId}}"
                                        aria-label="Remove {{this.name}} from cart"
                                    >
                                        <svg class="icon icon-sm"><use href="#icon-x"></use></svg>
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                                <a href="/products/{{this.handle}}" class="cart-item-name">
                                    {{this.name}}
                                </a>
                                <!-- Variant Options -->
                                {{#if this.variant}}
                                <div class="cart-item-variant">
                                    {{#if this.variant.shade}}

                                        <span
                                            class="cart-item-shade-swatch"
                                            style="background-color: {{this.variant.shadeColor}}"
                                        ></span>
                                        {{this.variant.shade}}
                                    </span>
                                    {{/if}}
                                    {{#if this.variant.size}}
                                    <span class="cart-item-size">{{this.variant.size}}</span>
                                    {{/if}}
                                </div>
                                {{/if}}
                                <!-- Quantity & Price -->

                                            type="button"
                                            class="cart-item-quantity-btn"
                                            data-action="decrease-quantity"
                                            data-line-id="{{this.lineId}}"
                                            aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                                            {{#if this.quantity === 1}}disabled{{/if}}
                                        >

                                        </button>

## 5.20 Print Styles & Debug Mode
```html
        <!-- ============================================
             PRINT STYLES (Inline for immediate availability)
        ============================================ -->
        <style media="print">
            /* Hide non-essential elements when printing */
            .header,
            .footer,
            .announcement-bar,
            .back-to-top,
            .chat-widget,
            .a11y-toggle-btn,
            .cookie-consent,
            .modal,
            .toast-container,
            .pwa-prompt,
            .ios-install-prompt,
            .offline-notification,
            .page-progress,
            .loading-overlay,
            .breadcrumb,
            .product-card-actions,
            .btn-icon,
            .social-share,
            video,
            iframe,
            [data-no-print],
            .no-print {
                display: none !important;
            }
            /* Reset backgrounds and colors */
            * {
                background: white !important;
                color: black !important;
                box-shadow: none !important;
                text-shadow: none !important;
            }
            /* Ensure links show URL */
            a[href]::after {
                content: " (" attr(href) ")";
                font-size: 0.8em;
                font-weight: normal;
            }
            a[href^="#"]::after,
            a[href^="javascript:"]::after {
                content: "";
            }
            /* Page breaks */
            h1, h2, h3, h4 {
                page-break-after: avoid;
            }
            img, figure, table {
                page-break-inside: avoid;
            }
            /* Reset container */
            .container {
                max-width: 100% !important;
                padding: 0 !important;
            }
            /* Product details print optimization */
            .product-gallery {
                width: 40% !important;
                float: left;
            }
            .product-details {
                width: 55% !important;
                float: right;
            }
            /* Order confirmation print styles */
            .order-confirmation {
                padding: 20px;
            }
            .order-items {
                border: 1px solid #ccc;
            }
            .order-total {
                font-weight: bold;
                border-top: 2px solid black;
            }
        </style>
        <!-- ============================================
             DEBUG MODE (Development Only)
        ============================================ -->
        {{#if (eq config.environment 'development')}}

            class="debug-panel"

                    <dt>Tier:</dt>

        opacity var(--transition-fast),
        visibility var(--transition-fast),
        transform var(--transition-fast);
}

    content: attr(data-tooltip);
    bottom: calc(100% + 8px);
    left: 50%;
    transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(4px);
    padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3);
    background-color: var(--color-primary);
    color: var(--color-white);
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    white-space: nowrap;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
}

    content: '';
    bottom: calc(100% + 4px);
    left: 50%;
    transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(4px);
    border: 4px solid transparent;
    border-top-color: var(--color-primary);
}

[data-tooltip]:hover::after,

    opacity: 1;
    visibility: visible;
    transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0);
}
